# Graph Report - rayforge  (2026-09-01)

## Corpus Check
- 2053 files · ~1,964,767 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 40468 nodes · 74382 edges · 1544 communities (1272 shown, 272 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 2897 edges (avg confidence: 0.93)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `4c3ea113`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
